a man who heads a monarchy; in an absolute monarchy, the supreme ruler of his nation."],"tags":["countable"]},{"glosses":["A male monarch; a man who heads a monarchy; in an absolute monarchy, the supreme ruler of his nation.","The monarch with the most power and authority in a monarchy, regardless of sex."],"tags":["countable"]},{"glosses":["A male monarch; a man who heads a monarchy; in an absolute monarchy, the supreme ruler of his nation.","A male leader of a traditional Aboriginal group, often used as a title by colonists."],"tags":["Australia","countable","historical"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Howard Stern styled himself as the \"king of all media\".","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A male monarch; a man who heads a monarchy; in an absolute monarchy, the supreme ruler of his nation.","A powerful or majorly influential person; someone who holds the preeminent position."],"tags":["countable","figuratively"]},{"examples":[{"text":"In times of financial panic, cash is king.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A male monarch; a man who heads a monarchy; in an absolute monarchy, the supreme ruler of his nation.","Something that has a preeminent position."],"tags":["countable","figuratively","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I'm sure you got the job, king.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A male monarch; a man who heads a monarchy; in an absolute monarchy, the supreme ruler of his nation.","A man worthy of respect."],"tags":["countable","slang"]},{"glosses":["A component of certain games.","The principal chess piece, that players seek to threaten with unavoidable capture to result in a victory by checkmate. It is often the tallest piece, with a symbolic crown with a cross at the top."],"topics":["board-games","chess","games"]},{"glosses":["A component of certain games.","A playing card with the letter \"K\" and the image of a king on it, the thirteenth card in a given suit."],"topics":["card-games","games"]},{"glosses":["A component of certain games.","A checker (a piece of checkers/draughts) that reached the farthest row forward, thus becoming crowned (either by turning it upside-down, or by stacking another checker on it) and gaining more freedom of movement."]},{"glosses":["A component of certain games.","The central pin or skittle in bowling games."]},{"glosses":["Senses related to animals","A male dragonfly;
